# Changelog

All notable changes to this workspace configuration are documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.0.0/).

---

## [Unreleased]

### Added
- **[2026-08-29]**: feat(co-deck): **`handbook-sync-audit` skill + `handbook-sync-audit.ts` 1.0.0 — post-publication handbook drift audit.** Three audit modes in one self-contained bun script: `content` (reflects `docs/designs` + `docs/adr` coverage in a handbook via keyword quorum, `--since` filter), `structure` (broken internal + cross-handbook links and orphan pages across all handbooks under a root), and `parity` (h2/id-set diff across ko/en/ja/es variants reporting exact missing sections). Registered in co-deck `skills/SKILLS.md`, `variant.json` skill manifest (owner: handbook-reviewer, phase H-6), `scripts/co-deck/SCRIPTS.md`, and distributed to `.claude`/`.gemini`/`.agents` platform skill dirs. Validated against `Handbooks/` (structure + parity PASS; content findings triaged → AGPL license rollout reflected into chapter 11 of multi-agent-harness-handbook).
- **[2026-08-29]**: feat(graph): **ADR-0060 Amendment 8 — agent co-use `composes_with` derivation + project backfill mode.** `tests/add-variant-relations.ts` gains `--project <path>` mode (recursive procedure walk incl. entity/region overrides; inline `[a, b]` and block `required_skills` both parsed; CRLF-normalized) and the Amendment 8 derivation: skills co-declared in one agent's `required_skills` gain a symmetric `composes_with`, declared on both sides. Applied: co-newbiz coverage 52 → 72/125 skills (+400 edges; 232n/1,029e), co-architect 4 → 10/32 (+9 edges); both graphs verify clean, co-newbiz's `graph-map --check` bidirectional contract holds; two legacy bare-string `relates_to` entries normalized to typed form. ADR-0060 Amendment 8 section added.

### Fixed
- **[2026-08-29]**: fix(upgrade): **`upgrade-project.ts` 1.17.1 → 1.17.2 — equal-version core-script drift reconciliation.** SYNC_IF_NEWER skipped scripts whose version matched the project's even when content differed, so locally forked core scripts stayed invisible to every future upgrade (audit across Projects/co-* found drifted forks in 7 of 8 projects, e.g. `sync-md`, `team-builder`, `dispatch`, `gen-pr-body`). Same-version scripts are now hash-compared and the canonical L1 copy is restored with a `⚠️ DRIFT … restored to canonical` notice. This closes the promotion-integrity hole: drifted core scripts hard-fail the L3→variant pipeline's integrity check.

### Fixed
- **[2026-08-29]**: fix(upgrade): **`upgrade-project.ts` 1.17.0 → 1.17.1 — country-prune safety for manifest-declared skills.** The country-scoped prune pass deleted skills registered in the workspace `country_scoped_assets` registry whenever the project's detected country didn't match — including skills the project's own `variant.json` `skill_manifest.variant_specific` deliberately declares (surfaced when co-newbiz, lacking `template-version.txt`, would have had `k-law` — a manifest-declared KR asset — silently pruned). The prune now KEEPS manifest-declared skills with a `KEEP` notice; manifest adoption beats country inference. Plus project-side hardening in `Projects/co-newbiz`: `template-version.txt` created (`variant=co-newbiz, country=KR` — upgrades now run common-only mode and preserve KR assets), `docs/countries/ACTIVE.md` pointer added, `k-dart`/`k-kosis` added to the skill manifest alongside `k-law`, and a dangling `translate → documentation-writing` edge removed.

### Fixed
- **[2026-08-29]**: fix(registry): **`verify-scripts.ts` 1.4.1 → 1.4.2 — recursive governance for variant-specific scripts (`scripts/co-*/`).** The file walker skipped any top-level `co-*` directory on the assumption of a separate `scripts/<variant>/SCRIPTS.md` sub-registry that no project actually has — so variant scripts nested in subdirectories (e.g. `scripts/co-newbiz/lib/*.ts`) silently escaped registration/version checks. Now the skip applies only when the variant directory genuinely carries its own `SCRIPTS.md` sub-registry; otherwise the walker recurses and the existing `co-*/…` relative-path registry rows (already the convention) govern nested files too. Verified across all 6 Projects/co-* registries; probe test confirms an unregistered nested file now fails the gate. Delivered to projects via the common-only upgrade path (`upgrade-project.ts 1.17.0`).

### Added
- **[2026-08-29]**: feat(upgrade): **Identity-separated fork support — `upgrade-project.ts` 1.16.0 → 1.17.0.** `Projects/co-architect` (scaffolded from the co-work variant template) is now managed as a distinct `co-architect` variant by user decision: `template-version.txt` re-pointed to `variant=co-architect`, procedure corpus (`procedure_id`, `variant`, step targets), `docs/phase-definitions.md`, skill `scope:` fields, and the placeholder `docs/co-work.context.md` all renamed/removed; `variant.json` gains a `derivedFrom` provenance field. To support this, `upgrade-project.ts` accepts a project whose `variant.json` `name` self-declares a variant with no `templates/<variant>/` directory in **common-only sync mode** (templates/common + project-owned files; variant-template passes and the template readiness gate no-op) — the project stops silently re-inheriting co-work's variant-specific assets while the `co-architect` template does not exist (its promotion is separately on hold). Documented in `docs/project-upgrade-guide.md` §Identity-Separated Forks.

### Added
- **[2026-08-29]**: feat(governance): **Promotion hold gate — variant promotion now requires explicit user approval.** `Projects/co-newbiz` and `Projects/co-architect` declare `promotionHold: { hold: true }` in `variant.json` (plus a ⛔ PROMOTION HOLD banner in each `PROMOTION_CHECKLIST.md`) by standing directive. Enforcement: `l3-to-variant-pipeline.ts` 1.13.0 → 1.14.0 gains a Phase 0.5 pre-flight that reads the SOURCE project's variant.json before any write and aborts on a hold (no `--force` bypass — Phase 8's readiness gate alone ran on the generated copy after the workspace tree was already modified); `project-to-variant.ts` 1.2.1 → 1.3.0 gains the identical pre-flight; `validate-variant-readiness.ts` 1.0.0 → 1.1.0 reports the hold as a blocking `promotion-hold` error; `promote-variant` skill 1.2.1 → 1.3.0 documents that green readiness checks are not an approval. Removing the hold is a deliberate owner edit to `variant.json` after the user approves in plain language. Design: docs/designs/2026-08-29-promotion-hold-gate-design.md.

### Fixed
- **[2026-08-29]**: fix(pipeline): **Project asset allowlist gate in upgrade-project + .bat nul-lint exemption (Projects/co-* upgrade wave follow-up).** (1) `upgrade-project.ts` 1.15.0 → 1.16.0: SYNC_IF_NEWER add-if-missing of common skills/agents now consults the project's `variant.json` (`skill_manifest.allowlist` / `agents[].file`) and SKIPS unregistered NEW assets instead of injecting them — the 0.6.0 i18n wave (`i18n-specialist` agent, `i18n-audit` skill) had landed in 3 projects whose variant rosters never registered them, tripping their `audit-variant` allowlist/parity checks. Existing-file updates stay ungated; the variant-skills pass is exempt (variant template is the authority); projects without `variant.json` remain ungated. (2) `audit.ts` 2.27.0 → 2.28.0: the banned `> nul` redirect lint no longer scans `.bat`/`.cmd` — cmd.exe `>nul` redirects to the NUL device and cannot materialize a file; flagging the idiomatic Windows batch redirect was a false positive (co-architect `setup.bat`). (3) Registry hygiene: `validate-procedures.ts` row added to `templates/common/scripts/SCRIPTS.md` with layer `L0+L1` (ships to projects via the PROCEDURES SYNC pass; missing row tripped lifecycle Check B). Design: docs/designs/2026-08-29-upgrade-asset-allowlist-gate-design.md.

### Added
- **[2026-08-29]**: feat(skill-graph): **`generate-skill-graph.ts` 1.7.1 → 1.8.3 — upstreamed the three co-newbiz fork adaptations so scaffolded projects no longer need a local generator fork.** (1) L0/L3 run-context detection now keys on `templates/common` instead of `templates/`, so projects carrying content template dirs (e.g. `templates/deliverables/`) are no longer mis-tagged L0. (2) New Source 3b reads a scaffolded project's own `variant.json` `skill_manifest`, materializing `used_by`/`phase` edges that previously only fired for `templates/co-*` manifests at the L0 root. (3) Agent discovery skips `README*.md` and `_*` files under `agents/` (phantom agent nodes in projects with folder READMEs). Propagated to `templates/common/scripts/`; co-newbiz's local fork replaced with the L0 copy (logic diff 0). L0 + all 14 scope graphs regenerated and verified 0-drift; co-newbiz project graph regenerated and verified (223 nodes / 661 edges).

### Added
- **[2026-08-29]**: feat(procedures): **Procedures guaranteed at every project lifecycle boundary (reledgev Addendum 3).** (1) `upgrade-project.ts` 1.14.0 → 1.15.0: new PROCEDURES SYNC pass — add-if-missing delivery of the variant procedure corpus (`procedures/` entries with project-owned preservation, `_output-types.yaml` seeding). (2) `scan-l3-project.ts`: new `procedures` scan category (`.yaml/.yml/.ts`) so the L3→variant promotion carries the workflow corpus. (3) `generate-skill-graph.ts` 1.7.0 → 1.7.1: namespace fix — procedure node ids now derive from `procedure_id` (strip trailing dirname) instead of the caller fallback, so project-local runs produce `procedure.co-abap.<name>` matching relations[] targets instead of ghost `l0`-namespace orphans; workspace graph unchanged at 542 nodes / 1,545 edges (identity-preserving), all 5 Projects/co-* graphs now verify with procedure nodes + step edges. (4) `tests/reflect-skill-graph-to-project.ts` extended with procedures adoption + context.md §Procedures; adopted into co-abap/co-consult/co-deck/co-price/co-safety (PRs #112/#21/#65/#76/#124, all merged).

### Fixed
- **[2026-08-29]**: fix(skills): **Propagation-coupling fix during Projects/co-* adoption wave.** Removed three relation edges whose targets are L0-only (do not propagate to L1/L2): `sync→audit-workspace`, `validate-docs-links→audit-workspace`, `team-builder→create-variant` — they dangled as unknown targets in every project copy. `validate-skills.ts` gains the `relation-target-not-in-l1` warning enforcing ADR-0060 Amendment 6B for the L1 surface; L0-only skill pairs are unaffected. New `tests/reflect-skill-graph-to-project.ts` mirrors the graph feature into Projects/co-* repos (scripts, selective SKILL.md relates_to refresh, context.md section, per-project adoption ADR, graph regeneration + determinism verification).

### Added
- **[2026-08-29]**: feat(pipeline): **Skill-graph regeneration at every lifecycle boundary + validators promoted to `/sync` gates (reledgev pipeline-coverage review).** (1) `l3-to-variant-pipeline.ts` 1.12.1 → 1.13.0: new Phase 6.5 regenerates and verifies the promoted variant's scope skill graph immediately after parity validation (non-fatal; previously deferred to the workspace's next `/sync`). (2) `upgrade-project.ts` 1.13.0 → 1.14.0: post-upgrade step regenerates the project-local `docs/skill-graph.json` right after the skills/agents refresh (non-fatal). (3) `dev-sync.ts` 1.7.8 → 1.8.0: new step 3.96 runs `validate-skills.ts` (relation metadata, fail-closed) and `validate-decisions.ts` (ADR-0061 chain, fail-closed) as sync gates — both were previously standalone-only. (4) Docs: `docs/constitution/06-skill-lifecycle.md` gains §6.2.1 "Skill Relation System (3-Layer Flexibility Model)" (layers, direction policy, pipeline integration); CONSTITUTION.md §6 hub gains the matching summary; `templates/common/docs/context.md` and `variant.context.template.md` gain a `## Skill Relationship Graph` section; `create-variant` / `promote-variant` skills gain skill-graph notes.

### Added
- **[2026-08-29]**: feat(graph): **Per-scope experimental override layer operational (reledgev §3 fully implemented; ADR-0060 Amendment 6 §D).** `generate-skill-graph.ts` 1.6.0 → 1.7.0: overrides loading/seed/application extracted into shared helpers and scope graphs (`buildScopeGraph`) now read `templates/<scope>/docs/skill-graph.overrides.json` in addition to the L0 `docs/` file (previously overrides were L0-only) — all 13 variant scopes carry seeded empty files; new `suppress: true` entries remove matching frontmatter-derived edges (`from` + `to`, `type` optional; override-vs-override suppression unsupported). `verify-skill-graph.ts` 1.4.0 → 1.5.0: override policy checks extracted into `checkOverridesFile()` and applied to scope overrides — `reason` and `since` are now required (missing → verification failure), `since` older than 90 days → warning prompting promotion to frontmatter or deletion, country-mark and endpoint-existence checks unchanged, legacy `last_reviewed` 12-month staleness kept for pre-reledgev entries. Functional tests verified on co-consult: override add, suppress removal, and the fail-closed missing-field path; all 14 scopes regenerated and verified 0-drift, L0 graph stable at 542 nodes / 1,548 edges.

### Added
- **[2026-08-29]**: docs(adr): **ADR-0060 Amendment 6 + ADR-0061 implementation-table sync for the `reledgev` waves.** ADR-0060 gained Amendment 6 recording the variant mass-adoption of typed `relates_to` (74 skills / 201 edges derived from the Procedure Schema corpus; consecutive steps → `follows`, non-consecutive co-use → symmetric `composes_with`; idempotent migration) and the normative direction policy (variant skill → L1 or same-variant targets only; variant-specific edges never written into L1 common skills; L0-only skills excluded as variant-edge targets) plus the Part 1c enforcement widening in `validate-skills.ts` 1.3.0. ADR-0061's Implementation table now records the landed fail-closed `scripts/validate-decisions.ts` (previously listed only as a follow-up-PR soft-check) with its check inventory and WARN→FAIL ladder positioning.

### Added
- **[2026-08-29]**: feat(skills): **Variant mass-adoption of typed `relates_to` (reledgev design addendum).** Extended typed skill relations to all variant templates: 74 variant skills gained typed `relates_to` (201 edges) derived mechanically from the Procedure Schema corpus via the idempotent migration script `tests/add-variant-relations.ts` — consecutive procedure steps become `follows`, non-consecutive co-used skills become symmetric `composes_with` (declared once from the alphabetically-first source); skills with pre-existing declarations only receive not-yet-declared edges. Direction policy (user-corrected): relations flow variant skill → L1 (`templates/common/skills/`) or same-variant targets; variant-specific edges are never written into L1 common skills, and L0-only sources are skipped. `validate-skills.ts` 1.2.0 → 1.3.0: Part 1c now scans every `templates/*/skills/` tree with recursive discovery (co-safety's nested `daily/<name>` / `domains/<name>` skills collected as slash-relative names). Result: 175 skills with typed relations; skill graph 542 nodes / 1,548 edges, determinism-verified; `audit.ts` passes. Design doc addendum records the wave and remaining deferrals.

### Added
- **[2026-08-29]**: feat(skills): **Skill Relation Formalization + Decision Chain Validator + I18N Audit Consolidation (`reledgev` design).** (1) `schemas/skill.schema.json` now formally defines `relates_to` (two homogeneous forms: legacy strings or typed `{skill, type}` objects — mixing rejected by the validator), `inputs`, `outputs` per ADR-0060 Amendment 3; `validate-skills.ts` 1.1.0 → 1.2.0 gains Part 1c relation validation (form homogeneity, relation-type vocabulary `relates_to|composes_with|follows|enables`, target existence across L0 + all variant templates, self-reference rejection). (2) 15 L0 lifecycle/governance skills gained typed `relates_to` centered on the L0 procedure chain (create-variant→promote-variant→project-to-variant→upgrade-project) plus governance pairings — skill graph regenerated to 542 nodes / 1,366 edges, determinism-verified. (3) New `scripts/validate-decisions.ts` (v1.0.0, fail-closed) validates DEC records per ADR-0061: required frontmatter, id/filename alignment, status vocabulary, `evidence_refs` ⊆ evidence-ledger row IDs, `knowledge_refs` existence, `rules_applied` ID format, `skills_used` resolution — all 4 existing DEC records pass. (4) I18N audit consolidation: new common skill `templates/common/skills/i18n-audit` (master-locale key parity, glossary adherence, L10N parity certificate) generalized from the co-price skill; co-price `i18n-audit` 2.0.0 → 2.1.0 re-anchored as a variant specialization; `i18n-specialist` agent gains the audit route, a coverage matrix (encoding/collation/notation/paper-size covered here; legal & trade routed out per ADR-0057/0058), and `required_skills` + `i18n-audit`. (5) Design doc `docs/designs/2026-08-29-relation-graph-evolution-and-decision-chain-design.md` records the three-layer relation flexibility model (frontmatter / overrides / derived projection), override usage rules with a 90-day review warning, and deferred work (variant commonization excluded by user decision; Rule-ID registry rollout; suggest-skill-relations; doc auto-regeneration design).

### Added
- **[2026-08-29]**: docs(adr): **ADR-0063 — Procedure Schema as Canonical Workflow Source.** Records the accepted decision for the procedure mechanism (procedure YAML as canonical source with graph as generated projection, L1–L8 repository-consistency validation, (agent, phase) step-level coverage with human-judged gaps and idempotent coverage_key tickets, workflow-shaped procedure design, and the L0 self-application with five lifecycle procedures). Linked from governance docs: constitution §6.7 (06.7-procedure-lifecycle.md), both procedure design documents; `verify-adr-governance.ts --strict` passes with zero linkage findings. Coverage design doc gained a post-merge addendum recording the 2026-08-29 phase-declaration review (3 under-declarations corrected, 3 coverable gaps closed, 17 continuous/cross-cutting pairs retired as N/A_JUSTIFIED) and making agent `phases:` frontmatter normative as the coverage requirement source.

### Added
- **[2026-08-29]**: feat(procedures): **Procedure Coverage & L0 Graph Integration — procedures promoted to first-class workflow objects; the procedure schema feature now applies to the workspace root itself.** New design doc `docs/designs/2026-08-29-procedure-coverage-and-l0-design.md` with 6 normative rules ((agent_key, phase)-unit step-level coverage; gaps are human judgment targets PROCEDURE_REQUIRED/N/A_JUSTIFIED, never auto-generated; idempotent `--tickets` via deterministic `coverage_key`; L0 Lifecycle Coverage split from Variant Coverage; workflow-shaped procedures forbid agent×phase decomposition; canonical-source invariant inherited). (1) **L0 skill-graph integration**: new root `procedures/` with 5 lifecycle procedures (`l0-create-variant`, `l0-promote-variant`, `l0-project-to-variant`, `l0-upgrade-project`, `l0-de-commonization-review`) typed-relation-chained and mapped 1:1 to the existing pipeline scripts; `validate-procedures.ts` 1.0.1 → 1.1.0 gains the root `l0` namespace (`l0-` prefix, root agents/skills fallback); `buildGraph` derives l0 procedure/output_type nodes. (2) **Scope graph procedure integration**: `generate-skill-graph.ts` 1.5.0 → 1.6.0 extracts a shared `deriveProceduresFromDir()` used by both `buildGraph` and `buildScopeGraph`, so every variant's scope-local `docs/skill-graph.json` now contains its own procedure nodes. (3) **Coverage engine**: new `scripts/procedure-coverage.ts` prints per-variant `(agent, phase)` matrices plus L0 lifecycle checks; `--tickets` registers governance tickets idempotently by `coverage_key` via ticket-store. (4) **Workflow-driven procedure expansion**: co-abap/co-price/co-safety agents got `phases:` frontmatter declarations; 32 new workflow-shaped procedures authored across 10 variants (co-consult +4, co-deck +4, co-design +4, co-develop +4 with feature-implementation extended, co-export +4, co-game +4, co-hr +3, co-news +4, co-price +4, co-security +4 with threat-model-and-report extended, co-work +3, co-abap +3, co-safety +2) closing 108→20 coverage gaps; the remaining 20 cross-cutting pairs (security-monitor, stack-setup, version agent, phase-6 continuous retest) registered as N/A-judgment governance tickets T-20260829-001..020. Graph now 538 nodes / 1334 edges, 65 procedures, deterministic regeneration and semantic regression verified. (5) **Governance reflection**: new `docs/constitution/06.7-procedure-lifecycle.md`, CONSTITUTION.md §6.7 header, context.md "Procedure Graph" section, variant.context.template.md `## Procedures` section, 06-skill-lifecycle cross-reference, and create-variant/ticket-run skill guidance ("repetition → scripts; judgment → guidelines + tickets").

### Added
- **[2026-08-29]**: feat(procedures): **Procedure Schema v1.0 — structured procedure pipeline (Procedure → Validation → Graph → Verification) across all 13 variant templates, generalizing the co-newbiz precedent.** New design doc `docs/designs/2026-08-29-procedure-schema-design.md` fixing 5 normative invariants (canonical-source: procedure YAML is the graph's source of truth, never hand-edited; outputs/inputs/relations independence; steps as canonical graph source; single `produces`-edge rule; regression ≠ determinism). New author-facing spec `templates/common/docs/procedure-schema-spec.md` + authoring skeleton `templates/common/procedures/_template/schema.yaml`. New validator `scripts/validate-procedures.ts` (L1 YAML/shape → L2 fields/enums → L3 output-type vocabulary → L4 agent refs → L5 skill refs (nested `a/b` keys supported) → L6 relation type → L7 relation target → L8 cross-file consistency), fixture-tested in `tests/validate-procedures.test.ts` (9 pass incl. exit-code path) with fixtures under `tests/procedures-fixtures/`. Seeded `procedures/_output-types.yaml` + one domain-grounded procedure schema per all 13 variants (`co-abap` … `co-work`; co-safety keeps its existing `workflows/` untouched). `generate-skill-graph.ts` 1.4.0 → 1.5.0: new `procedure`/`output_type` node types and `step_uses_skill`/`step_by_agent`/`produces` edge types derived from procedure schemas. `verify-skill-graph.ts` 1.2.0 → 1.3.0: orphan-procedure detection, procedure-edge endpoint checks, and `--determinism` mode. Verified: validator all-variant pass, graph regenerated (13 procedure nodes, 46 step_uses_skill / 46 step_by_agent / 47 produces edges), semantic regression clean (all 296 pre-existing nodes / 669 edges preserved), determinism check pass, bun test 9/9.

### Changed
- **[2026-08-29]**: docs(adr): **ADR-0060 Amendment 5 — procedure-derived nodes and edges.** Records the parent-workspace adoption of the inferential strategy recognized by Amendment 4: procedure schemas (`templates/<variant>/procedures/` + root `l0` namespace) are now a first-class edge source of `generate-skill-graph.ts`, deriving `procedure`/`output_type` nodes and `step_uses_skill`/`step_by_agent`/`produces` edges (INV-4 single produces-edge rule) into both the L0 graph and each variant's scope-local graph. Extends Amendment 4's normative rule with canonical-source discipline for procedure-derived artifacts (`source: procedure_schema`, overrides-forbidden), documents the verification evidence at adoption (541 nodes / 1343 edges, 65 procedures, semantic regression + determinism green), and positions the change relative to Amendment 4's three-stage co-newbiz generalization criterion. Decision of record: ADR-0063.

### Changed
- **[2026-08-29]**: fix(procedures): **phase declaration review across all 13 variants — truthful frontmatter, 3 more coverage pairs closed, resolved tickets retired.** Corrected under-declared agent phases to match actual workflow participation (`co-develop code-writer` [4]→[3,4], `co-game game-developer` [4]→[3,4], `co-abap test-runner` [4]→[3,4] — the extra pairs were already covered by existing procedure steps). Closed 3 remaining judgment gaps with workflow-natural steps: co-design `visual-implementation` gained a design-lead review step (design-lead@3), co-hr `hr-solution-design` gained a labor-relations impact step (@2), co-game `game-concept-design` gained a prototyping-environment step (stack-setup@1). Resolved coverage tickets T-20260829-009/014/015 moved to done; remaining 17 uncovered pairs are all continuous/cross-cutting roles (security-monitor 0/5, stack-setup@0, version 0–6, co-security phase-6 retest) legitimately kept as N/A_JUSTIFIED tickets. Validator all-namespace pass, coverage re-run clean, graph regenerated and determinism-verified.

### Changed
- **[2026-08-29]**: feat(scripts): **drift-prevention checks — 3 new guards close the loops that required manual re-sync today.** (1) `validate-skills.ts` 1.0.3 → 1.1.0, new **Part 1b layer-placement check** (workspace-root only): any `skills/*/SKILL.md` whose `scope` names a variant FAILs with a move-to-templates fix — extends the B-11 common-leak guard to the L0 side, which previously had no detector. (2) `verify-skills.ts` 1.2.0 → 1.3.0, new **SKILLS.md catalog-sync check**: every `skills/*/SKILL.md` must have a catalog row with a matching version (FAIL on drift) — the 12-item catalog drift found in the audit can no longer accumulate silently. Also fixed a pre-existing runtime breakage: `scanSkills()` used `Bun.glob`, unavailable in the current Bun runtime, so the script crashed on every run; replaced with recursive `readdirSync`. The legacy `generateSkillsIndex()` auto-writer is now guarded — it no longer overwrites the curated lifecycle-registry catalog (it only writes the legacy `# Skills Index` stub format). (3) `validate-templates.ts` 1.19.0, new **C-CM-03**: `common-contract.json` `common_skills` versions must match `templates/common/skills/` frontmatter — immediately caught 5 more stale contract entries (`project-review` 1.1.0, `meeting-facilitation` 1.4.0, `team-builder` 1.1.0, `evidence-ledger` 1.1.0, `decision-record` 1.1.0), all fixed. SCRIPTS.md rows + L1 mirrors synced (71 L0/L1 pairs in sync). Negative-fixture tested: a fake `scope: co-fake` skill in skills/ correctly FAILs Part 1b and passes after removal. Verified: validate-skills 0/0, verify-skills 0 failed, validate-templates 0 errors (C-CM-03 all-pass), lifecycle-sync-audit all-pass, audit all-pass.
- **[2026-08-29]**: docs(governance): **documentation alignment for the skill-layer restructure — stale L0+L1+L2 skill notation purged, ADR-0032 Amendment 1, hygiene design doc superseded note + late spec registration.** Closes the doc-vs-code gaps from the de-commonization work: (1) CONSTITUTION §6.2 `scope` definition now states variant-scoped skills resolve to **L0+L2** (variant-exclusive, owned-variant-only, no L0/common base per DEC-20260829-02, layer-filter 1.5.0) — the old "propagates to L0+L1+L2" sentence and the `variant` placeholder scope value are gone; the field-name note clarifies L0+L1+L2 remains scripts-only. (2) Both SKILLS.md catalogs: header note rewritten to the L0+L2 variant-exclusive model. (3) **ADR-0032 Amendment 1** appended: amended Structural Rules table for skills (skills/ = L0/L0+L1 only, common = L0+L1 only, co-*/skills/ = L0+L2 only), amended decision-tree Q4 (author variant-exclusive skills directly in templates/co-*/skills/, no base-for-later), script layer model explicitly unchanged. No promotion decision made; both the declarative pilots and this experiment stand as recorded data points per ADR-0060 Amendment 4's three-stage revisit criterion. - **[2026-08-29]**: docs(adr): **ADR-0060 Amendment 4 — recognize the inference-derived graph strategy (co-newbiz precedent)** (design `docs/designs/2026-08-29-inference-derived-graph-strategy-design.md`, documentation-only, user-directed counter-proposal). A proposed "Phase 1.5" experiment (apply Amendment 3's declarative `relates_to` schema to real workflows) targeted `Projects/co-newbiz`; investigation found the premise didn't fit — co-newbiz has its own independently-designed, already-complete graph system (`scripts/co-newbiz/graph.ts` v0.4.0) that infers 7 node types and 12 edge types purely from existing structured SSOTs (procedure schemas, kill-criteria rule predicates, decision docs), declaring zero new `SKILL.md` frontmatter. This matures exactly what the 2026-08-25 Document Layer amendment deferred ("revisit on first generic registry"). Amendment 4 records that ADR-0060's principle (generated projection of SSOTs, never hand-maintained) admits two valid strategies — **Declarative** (Amendment 3, frontmatter is the SSOT) and **Inferential** (Amendment 4, existing structured data is the SSOT) — and adds the load-bearing normative rule: "An inferential graph MUST NOT introduce a second hand-maintained declaration of facts already authoritative elsewhere." Also records: provenance-by-construction (co-newbiz's `source_ref` is inherent in the extraction event, unlike Amendment 3's bolted-on `provenance` field), a forward reference to co-newbiz's `context --skill`/`context --gate` commands as a working precedent for graph-to-agent-context consumption (Phase 4 material, not built now), and a three-stage revisit criterion (one project → no action; second independent project → compare extraction contracts; common contract confirmed → consider an opt-in generic extraction mode in `generate-skill-graph.ts`) so "a second project exists" alone doesn't trigger premature generalization. co-newbiz is recorded as recognized precedent, not promoted standard. Zero code changes — no `.ts`/`SKILL.md` files touched, nothing inside `Projects/co-newbiz` modified.
- **[2026-08-29]**: feat(graph): **typed relation vocabulary + scaffold-time project graph generation** (design `docs/designs/2026-08-28-skill-graph-typed-relations-design.md`, ADR-0060 Amendment 3, user-approved via 3-option AskUserQuestion; Phase 1 of a 5-phase roadmap). Two gaps closed: `relates_to` was schema-only (zero skills used it) with no typed vocabulary, and project graphs were generated lazily at the first `/sync` instead of at scaffold time. **`generate-skill-graph.ts` 1.3.0 → 1.4.0**: `relates_to` now accepts either the legacy bare-string-array form (unchanged) or an array of typed `{skill, type}` objects using three new edge types — `composes_with` (symmetric, stored as one directed edge carrying `symmetric: true`, consumers MUST traverse both ways), `follows` (pure sequencing, no dependency implication), `enables` (A's output unlocks B, not the inverse of `requires`); mixing bare strings and typed objects in the same `relates_to` array is a schema-validation error (`"relates_to must contain either all string entries or all typed relation objects; mixed entries are not allowed"`), not a YAML-parse error — the array itself is valid YAML. New optional `inputs`/`outputs` frontmatter fields (opaque labels, not artifact identity — avoids pre-constraining Phase 4's artifact-node promotion), rendered per-skill in `docs/skill-graph.md`. Every edge sourced from SKILL.md `prerequisites`/`relates_to` and agent `required_skills` now carries `provenance: {file, field, index?}` (JSON-only). `parseFrontmatter()` swapped from the hand-rolled single-line regex parser to `js-yaml` (already a dependency — no new package added), scoped strictly to the `---`/`---` block; falls back to the legacy line parser for a handful of pre-existing `docs/decisions/DEC-*.md` frontmatter blocks that are not strict YAML (out of this pass's scope). **`verify-skill-graph.ts` 1.1.0 → 1.2.0**: `relates_to` country-mark/unknown-target checks now go through the real parser + shared `parseRelatesTo()` instead of a line-regex scan that mis-tokenized typed multi-line entries. **`new-project.ts` 1.8.0 → 1.9.0**: new scaffold-time step generates `docs/skill-graph.json` (tagged `L3`) inside the new project directory immediately after dependency install, non-fatal warn-and-continue (the existing `dev-sync.ts` step 4.65 gate still covers it on first `/sync` if scaffold-time generation is skipped) — projects still never receive a copy of any upstream graph. Applied the typed `relates_to`/`inputs`/`outputs` shape as a proof-of-concept to 4 co-consult skills (`executive-presentation`, `technical-feasibility`, `org-readiness-assessment`, `financial-modeling`) — not a mass migration of the ~164-skill catalog. Parser-swap regression check surfaced 4 previously-silently-dropped edges (multi-line YAML list frontmatter the old regex parser couldn't parse, e.g. `i18n-specialist`'s `required_skills:` list) as a net-positive correctness fix, not a regression — 0 edges lost. Post-review fix: `technical-feasibility` and `financial-modeling` had each independently declared `composes_with` on the other, materializing two redundant symmetric edges for one relationship — the convention is "declare once, either side"; removed the duplicate from `technical-feasibility`. L0 graph regenerated (294 nodes, 664 edges, 0 unexpected drift); all 14 template scope graphs regenerated and verified 0 drift. ADR-0060 gains Amendment 3 recording the typed vocabulary, the scaffold-time decision, a forward-open-schema/unknown-key-tolerance/SSOT/three-governance-units evolution boundary for Phase 2+, and the full Phase 2–5 roadmap (Governance/Migration/Intelligence/Audit) as documentation only. Verified: parser fixture tests (legacy/typed/mixed-rejected/unknown-key-tolerated + frontmatter-only-scope), scaffold-time smoke test (throwaway project, `docs/skill-graph.json` present pre-`/sync` tagged L3), `bun scripts/audit.ts` 0 errors, `bun scripts/validate-templates.ts` 0 errors, `lifecycle-sync-audit.ts` inside a scaffolded project (caught and required the SCRIPTS.md version-bump gate this pass satisfies). `/sync` intentionally not run — local verification only, per task scope.

Investigation showed `agents/domains/` in the template was already correctly nested — only `skills/` had been flattened, apparently by a later `variant regen` pass using older flat-assuming logic; the template's own manifest/doc references were internally consistent with the flat layout (not a partial/broken state), so this was a genuine two-way fork rather than a bug in one side. Updated the resulting path references in `agents/domains/industry/gmp/gmp-agent.md`, `variant.json`, and `templates/co-safety/scripts/test-pharma-general-profile.ts` (now byte-identical to the project's copy). 6 skills present only in the template with no project counterpart (`api-documentation`, `documentation-writing`, `finishing-a-development-branch`, `platform-command-lifecycle-manager`, `platform-skill-lifecycle-manager`, `research-analysis`) were left flat, unclassified — they don't exist in `templates/common/skills/` either, so their origin/ownership is unclear and out of scope for this restructuring. Two validator scripts assumed a flat skill layout and needed nested-aware fixes: `scripts/validate-templates.ts`'s WS-06/B-09 checks (`checkVariantSkillsLayer`/`checkVariantSkills`) did a single-level `readdirSync` and flagged every category directory (`daily`, `domains`, `investigation`, `emergency`) as "SKILL.md missing" or "not declared L0+L1+L2" — added a shared `collectSkillDirs()` recursive walker (skill = first directory found containing a direct `SKILL.md`, category dirs are walked into instead of flagged); `scripts/helpers/layer-filter.ts`'s `parseSkillLayers()`/`_parseSkillLayersFromFrontmatter()` had the same single-level assumption — made recursive, keying each skill by both its full relative path and bare basename (preserves existing flat-layout callers). Verified: `bun scripts/audit.ts` 0 errors, `bun scripts/validate-templates.ts` 0 errors (WS-06/B-09 false positives cleared), `bun scripts/validate-pm-extends.ts` 28 files 0 errors, `bun scripts/test-platform-parity.ts` PASS, and all 5 `templates/co-safety/scripts/test-*.ts` suites (pharma-general-profile 10/10, domain-scenarios 90/90, cross-domain-integration 8/8, runtime-tools 20/20, chemical-handling-profile 12/12).

The new §20 addendum (4 subsections) records four findings verified by direct inspection of the co-newbiz repo: (1) web-stack doc drift — the ADR was recorded, but README/agent files kept describing the old stack after cutover (fixed directly in the co-newbiz repo); (2) the `agents/` directory being flat rather than the design's physical `_core/domains/_shared` tree is confirmed as a deliberate decision (ADR-0013/0043 plus `agents/README.md`'s "4 classes" contract), not an oversight; (3) the 3-stage-review persistence layer (`review_stage` table, `reviewer_domain` column) exists only as schema with no repository/API/UI consumer; (4) the human-final-approval table (`analysis_task.reviewed_by`) exists and is already read by the kill-criteria engine, but no code path ever writes to it, so that kill-criteria check currently passes vacuously. Item (1)'s drift was fixed directly in the co-newbiz repo; items (3)/(4)'s gaps were corrected/flagged in co-newbiz's `skills/agent-output-provenance/SKILL.md` (1.1.0). - **[2026-08-28]**: fix(scripts): `dev-sync.ts` 1.7.6 → 1.7.7 — step 3.95's QA pre-check ran bare `bun test` (no path) instead of the project's own `bun run test` (`package.json` → `bun scripts/test-runner.ts integration`, scoped to `tests/`). Bare `bun test` recursively scans the entire CWD tree for `*.test.ts`, which pulled in every nested `Projects/*/` repo's own test suite (Playwright visual-regression screenshots, MCP stdio integration tests, security-validator tests, etc.) — observed live turning this non-fatal check into a multi-minute detour full of unrelated failures on an otherwise-unrelated `/sync` run. ### Added
- **[2026-08-28]**: fix(scripts): follow-through on the co-safety template promotion-gap fix (see prior Fixed entry) — three remaining gaps closed. **(1)** `scripts/upgrade-project.ts` 1.10.1 → 1.11.0: new **VARIANT ASSET DIRS SYNC** pass generically discovers any top-level variant template directory not already covered by the existing agents/skills/scripts/docs passes (excludes `agents`, `skills`, `scripts`, `docs`, `.claude`, `.gemini`, `.agents`, `.git`, `.github`, `.githooks`, `memory`, `node_modules`) and hash-syncs new/changed files into the project, preserving project-only files. Previously `co-safety`'s `workflows/`, `regulations/`, `evidence-models/`, `industry-profiles/` (and any future variant's equivalent) had **no upgrade path at all** — a project scaffolded before the directory existed in the template, or missing it from a promotion gap, would never receive it via `upgrade-project`. Generic by design (not hardcoded to co-safety) so it covers any variant that grows a top-level asset directory. **(2)** `docs/workspace-schema.json` `country_scoped_assets` gains a new `dirs` registry category (`{ "regulations/KR": "KR" }`) and `scripts/helpers/prune-country-scoped-assets.ts` 0.2.0 → 0.3.0 gains a `pruneDir()` pass consuming it — closes the gap where `new-project.ts`'s reachable region-neutral path (`--yes` with no `--country`, or the interactive "Region-neutral" option) left KR-specific regulation YAMLs in a project that declared no target jurisdiction, with no pruning mechanism for directories (only skills/scripts/env keys were previously prunable). **(3)** New `tests/co-safety-template-completeness.test.ts` (9 tests): asserts `templates/co-safety`'s 4 asset directories exist and are non-empty (regression guard for the exact promotion gap this PR series fixed), validates the new `dirs` registry entries resolve to real template paths, exercises `prune-country-scoped-assets.ts`'s directory pruning both ways (region-neutral removes, KR-targeted keeps), and spawns `upgrade-project.ts --dry-run` against a minimal region-neutral co-safety project fixture to confirm all 4 directories are reported `NEW` and nothing is written in dry-run mode. Auto-discovered by `test-runner.ts`'s integration suite (`tests/*.test.ts` glob) — runs on every `/sync` pre-push. Verified: `bun scripts/test-runner.ts integration --parallel` → 6/6 files pass; manual smoke test of `pruneDir()` against temp fixtures (region-neutral removes `regulations/KR/`, keeps `regulations/international/`; KR-targeted keeps `regulations/KR/`).

- **[2026-08-25]**: feat(governance): **Evidence layer made real — `evidence-ledger` skill 1.0.0 → 1.1.0 gains the registry-backed overlay form, and the workspace's FIRST live ledger opens with four verified rows wired into both seeded decision records.** Gap #3 of the chain follow-through: the Evidence layer existed as a standard with zero instances. Three moves close it. **(1) Overlay forms codified**: the skill's Variant Overlays section now names the second legitimate overlay form alongside co-news's prose ledger — **registry-backed variants** carrying their evidence plane as formal variables in a schema-governed registry (row-per-VARIABLE, verification metadata, reversal via the registry's own amendment protocol), with the invariant that DEC `evidence_refs[]` must resolve INSIDE whichever form a variant uses. This retroactively legitimizes exactly how co-newbiz already operates. **(2) First live ledger**: `docs/evidence/ledger.md` opens with four VERIFIED rows (EV-20260825-001…004) documenting this week's real rulings and findings — the rootAllowlist stray-file gate firing, the direct-commit denial → /sync routing, consumes-inference precision 1/1 corroborated by a kill-criteria predicate, and the human-role registry resolution — each with source, ref, and verification method. **(3) DEC loop closed in practice**: DEC-20260825-01/02 `evidence_refs[]` now cite EV-001/EV-002 instead of empty arrays — the first decision records whose evidence citations resolve to a real ledger. Platform mirrors refreshed by the sync pipeline; no contract pins affected.
- **[2026-08-25]**: revert(governance): **ADR seeds and Variant Anchor sections removed from templates — owner review found the application problematic (#691 follow-up; rationale recorded in `docs/audits/2026-08-25-…md` §5).** Three defects acknowledged: the seed README duplicated the context.md convention subsection (two homes for one rule = ADR-0050's drift problem); "likely first records" taxed every future project with speculative guidance about engagements that have not happened; and the organic project practices (69/18/5 records, no seeds) had been misread as a seeding need when they proved the opposite — git tracks no empty directories, so `docs/adr/` materializes exactly when a first record exists. Removed: 11 variant `docs/adr/README.md`, common seed README, all Variant Anchor sections. Restored: co-develop `.gitkeep` (pre-existing), common `.gitkeep` (untouched). Survives: the **Architecture Decision Records** subsection in `templates/common/docs/context.md` — now the single home of the convention — and the audit notes document, which gains a §5 owner-review postscript so the reversal reasoning outlives the session.
- **[2026-08-25]**: feat(deps): **root→template dependency auto-sync landed — `sync-template-deps.ts` + dev-sync step 4.52 self-heal + audit mirror gate (automation follow-through on the manual mirror in the Changed section below).** (1) New `scripts/sync-template-deps.ts` (1.0.0, registry scope L0): pure core `alignTemplateDeps(rootPkg, templatePkg)` mutates the template package.json in place for **shared dep keys only** — root-only deps (e.g. `semver`, `@types/js-yaml`) are never added (the contract test requires `@types/js-yaml` absent since js-yaml 5.x ships built-in types), template-only deps are never removed (conservative; removal stays a manual decision), `engines` fields align when both sides define them, and comparison is exact version-string so range-format differences (`^5.3.0` vs `5.3.0`) count as drift. CLI: `--check` (default) reports drift and exits 1 without writing; `--apply` rewrites `templates/common/package.json` (2-space JSON + trailing LF) and regenerates `bun.lock` via `Bun.spawn(['bun','install'], {cwd: 'templates/common'})`, exiting 1 on install failure so a stale lock can never land silently. (2) `scripts/dev-sync.ts` 1.7.4 → 1.7.5: new step 4.52 (workspace-root gated, same shape as the propagate step) runs `--apply` between step 4.5 and 4.55 — the first `/sync` after any dependabot bump now heals the template automatically, with the healed files swept into the same commit by the step-6 `git add -A`. (3) `scripts/audit.ts` 2.22.0 → 2.23.0: new `checkTemplateDependencyMirror()` (modeled on `checkVariantJsonSchema()`) compares shared dep versions root↔template and FAILs on drift with the `--apply` heal command in the message; skips when `templates/common/package.json` is absent (L1/L3 contexts). (4) `scripts/lifecycle-sync-audit.ts` 1.4.8 → 1.4.9: two INTENTIONAL_CROSS_REFS whitelist entries for the L0-only script references inside the L1 mirrors (same house pattern as `dev-sync:propagate-to-templates`). (5) Registry: `scripts/SCRIPTS.md` + manually mirrored `templates/common/scripts/SCRIPTS.md` gain the `sync-template-deps.ts` row (scope L0 — propagate auto-skips the L1 copy) and a Guide subsection; both `scripts/README.md` files regenerated. L1 `.ts` mirrors for dev-sync/audit/lifecycle-sync-audit propagated via `--apply --domain scripts`. - **[2026-08-25]**: fix(pipeline): **cascade lag healed — new dev-sync step 4.62 unconditional re-publish after skill sync, gated like 4.5, with verified counter-pollution correction and test coverage.** Root cause: the cross-script chain `skills/` →(step 4.6 sync-skills)→ root platform dirs `.claude/.gemini/.agents/skills` →(step 4.5 propagate)→ template platform copies `templates/common/.claude|...` has 4.5 running **before** 4.6, so when a source skill changes, 4.5 compares stale platform copies, copies nothing, then 4.6 updates platforms — template copies heal only on the **next** sync. Observed in PR #694 (sync SKILL.md 1.2.1 lag, 3 stale template copies). Fix: new step 4.62 runs `propagate-to-templates.ts --apply` unconditionally **after** step 4.6 (full re-pass, no `--domain` flag), gated by workspace-root guard + L0-fatal/L1-warn split (mirrors step 4.5; propagate is L0-only). Three verified design corrections: (1) **copied-counter pollution** → line-based convergence assertion in test (`copied` lines only, NOT `scrubbed` lines, since scrubbed rewrites are counted but byte-identical by design); (2) **4.5-style gating** because propagate-to-templates.ts is L0-only (no propagation domain covers scripts); (3) **full re-pass** because `--domain` is single-valued. Files changed: `scripts/dev-sync.ts` 1.7.5→1.7.6 (+step 4.62 block), `tests/propagate-to-templates.test.ts` (+cascade convergence test, 1 new describe), `tests/unit/dev-sync-pipeline-order.test.ts` (NEW, 3 ordering tests), `skills/sync/SKILL.md` 1.2.1→1.2.2 (+step 4.62 row), `scripts/SCRIPTS.md` + `templates/common/scripts/SCRIPTS.md` (dev-sync row bumped), `docs/designs/2026-08-25-pipeline-cascade-repass-design.md` (NEW, design doc), `docs/specs/registry.json` (entry #63). The landing sync dogfoods the fix — new code runs from working tree, console GREEN line proves same-commit healing. English, Hangul-free.

- **[2026-08-25]**: feat(docs): **co-consult engagement kickoff artifact pack — PR28 (backlog §8 row 18, variant benchmarking: McKinsey-style issue tree + hypothesis log templates).** New `templates/co-consult/docs/kickoff-artifact-pack.md`: the two standard kickoff artifacts every engagement produces BEFORE fieldwork — an **issue-tree template** (root question discipline: one sentence, answerable, no conjunctions; 3-5 branches per node at one decomposition logic per level; every leaf a verifiable question, never "explore X"; per-branch sub-question/data-required/owner annotations) and a **hypothesis-log template** (7-column ID/hypothesis/tree-branch/test-method/status/evidence/date table; every hypothesis traces to a tree branch — no branch means scope creep; falsifiable phrasing required, tautologies banned; status open → supported/disproved/unresolvable with evidence-cited transitions; append-only audit trail — disproved hypotheses are never deleted; ISO 8601 dating). Wired into the engagement lifecycle via a 7-step kickoff flow (strategy-analyst drafts the tree → audits it via the `mece-logic-auditor` skill at blocker-clear gate → derives 3-7 initial hypotheses → pm approves both at the Phase 2 gate → Phase 1 analysts keep the log current with weekly pm drift review → strategy-analyst/data-analyst run `insight-synthesis` at the Phase 1.5 cross-validation checkpoint → communications-lead structures the report branch-per-branch via `consulting-report-writing`). Skill-relationship section maps the pack against mece-logic-auditor (audit gate), insight-synthesis (log consumer), consulting-report-writing (section-per-branch structure), stakeholder-alignment (tree approval as stakeholder checkpoint). Closes the "no engagement kickoff artifact pack (issue tree + hypothesis log templates)" gap — the audit and report skills existed, but no standard templates connected them to kickoff. - **[2026-08-25]**: feat(docs): **co-news corrections workflow — PR22 (backlog §9 row 11, variant benchmarking: Reuters-style published-corrections discipline).** New `templates/co-news/docs/corrections-workflow.md` (148 lines): published-corrections discipline for the co-news newsroom — every material error in published work is visible to the reader, never silently edited; transparency over face-saving is what distinguishes a correction from an unauthorized rewrite. Five-class error severity taxonomy (F1: factual/numerical error in substance; F2: attribution/quote error; F3: headline/summary overstates body; F4: non-substantive typo/formatting; F5: legal/defamation-risk error). Flagging intake (reporter self-report, fact-checker post-publish audit, reader/external report, visual-editor on graphic errors) lands in a single corrections queue owned by pm. Adjudication workflow: pm triages within 4 hours, fact-checker verifies errors against source-verification-ledger for F1/F2/F3, legal-researcher gates F5 errors with veto authority, response deadlines (F1/F2: same working day; F3: within 24h; F4: batched weekly; F5: immediate escalation). Correction protocol: in-place fix plus appended correction note with template (what was wrong, what is correct, correction date ISO 8601, adjudicator, class); headline corrections preserve original headline in note; substantive F1/F2 changes may quote original in correction note but never inline strikethrough in body. Corrections register at `docs/output/corrections-log.md` with columns (correction_date, article_slug, class, summary, adjudicator, resolution). Unpublish vs correct decision rule (correct in place with note by default for F1/F2/F3/F4; unpublish reserved for F5 legal-risk or fundamentally unsound sourcing, with placeholder note left behind). Source-verification-ledger integration: corrections update the evidence record for affected claims, re-verify NEWS-R1 two-source rule compliance, reporter accountability logged via self-report flag_source. Role summary table mapping all 7 co-news agents to corrections responsibilities (pm: queue owner/adjudicator; fact-checker: classification + audit + ledger updates; legal-researcher: F5 gate; reporter: self-report duty; style-editor: F4 batch; visual-editor: graphic correction; financial-analyst: numeric re-verification). No other files changed. - **[2026-08-25]**: feat(scripts): **co-game single-file HTML game bundler — PR19 (backlog §6 row 8, variant benchmarking: the roadmap Phase-2 distribution deliverable, shipped as the variant's first script).** New `templates/co-game/scripts/co-game/bundle-html.ts` (1.0.0): zero-dependency TypeScript (Bun/node stdlib only) bundler that turns a built game directory into ONE distributable HTML file — local `<script src>` inlined as inline blocks (non-src attributes preserved, `</script` sequences escaped as `<\/script` so JS bodies cannot terminate the block), local `<link rel="stylesheet">` inlined as `<style>` blocks with recursive local `@import` following and `url(...)` → data-URI resolution (paths relative to each declaring CSS file), and local img/audio/video/source `src` rewrites to base64 data URIs (mime map: png/jpg/jpeg/gif/webp/svg/ogg/mp3/wav/woff2; unknown extension → WARN + leave path). Remote http(s)/protocol-relative/schemed URLs, `data:` URIs, and fragment refs are left untouched and reported in a skipped-remote list; recursion guard (win32 case-normalized path stack seeded with the entry HTML) blocks circular/self inclusion; pass order stylesheets→media→scripts prevents inserted script strings from being re-scanned; idempotent via a generated `<!-- co-game bundle-html vX.Y.Z -->` marker after the doctype (re-running on the bundler's own output = "Already bundled" no-op, exit 0, nothing written); deterministic document-order output, path resolution relative to the entry HTML, UTF-8 no BOM. CLI: `bun scripts/co-game/bundle-html.ts --input <built-dir> [--output <file.html>] [--check] [--help]` — input defaults `./dist`, output defaults `<input>/game-bundle.html`, `--check` reports what would be inlined plus failures without writing; summary prints per-category inlined file/byte counts, input-tree vs bundle size, skipped-remote list, warnings; exit 1 with clear message on missing entry HTML or unreadable referenced file. Tests: new `templates/co-game/tests/bundle-html.test.ts` (7 tests, bun:test, OS-temp-dir fixtures: full inline pass incl. CSS url() and attribute preservation, remote/data-URI pass-through with reporting, idempotent no-op re-run, missing index.html exit 1, `--check` writes nothing with category counts, unknown-extension WARN leave-as-is, missing-asset exit 1 no output). - **[2026-08-24]**: feat(scripts): marker-based doc propagation — **PR7 of the 10-PR design series (marker-rewrite engine + 2 propagation domains + shared parser + README --regenerate + skill graph 202→203 nodes + fixture tests + lifecycle bookkeeping).** **D1 — marker-rewrite engine** (`propagate-to-templates.ts` 2.4.0 → 2.5.0): new `--marker-rewrite` mode composable with `--apply` upgrades detect-then-reseed to detect-then-REWRITE (engine writes, then re-hashes) — for every existing `<!-- COMMON-<DOMAIN>:START/END -->` zone in a target file: replaces the zone's content with the current source slice; for every `<!-- intentional-duplicate: <name>; source: <path>; hash: <sha256-8> -->` marker: replaces the section content with the current source slice AND refreshes the hash. Logs EVERY overwritten zone/marker to stdout before writing (file, marker name, +/- line counts); zones/markers left byte-identical → log "in sync". Preserves each file's existing line-ending style (CRLF vs LF detection per file) and writes with UTF-8 (no BOM). Contract note: local customization belongs OUTSIDE zones; content inside a marked zone is engine-owned and will be overwritten. **D2 — two new propagation domains** (`propagation-map.json`): `constitution-context` (CONSTITUTION.md section slices → templates/common/docs/context.md marker zones, reusing the existing scrubConstitutionRefs transform) + `variant-context` (templates/common/docs/context.md sections → marker zones in each pilot variant's docs/<variant>.context.md) — both domains mode marker-inject + rewrite, file-level whitelist, pilot scope owner-confirmed (co-news, co-hr, co-export, co-consult; remaining 7 variants convert after stabilization). **D3 — shared marker parser** (`helpers/markers.ts` 1.0.0): extracts marker-zone + intentional-duplicate parsing into a shared module imported by BOTH propagate-to-templates.ts and verify-adr-governance.ts — zero behavior change to verify-adr-governance (after refactor, `bun scripts/verify-adr-governance.ts --strict` still reports 9/9 markers in sync and exits 0 — this protects dev-sync step 3.97 from parser divergence per ADR-0062). Exports: findMarkerZones(), extractMarkerZones(), scanIntentionalDuplicateMarkers(), computeSectionHash(), resolveConstitutionSource(), extractSectionContent(). **D4 — README regeneration flag** (`helpers/generate-variant.ts` 1.12.0 → 1.13.0): new `--regenerate <variant>` re-runs the existing README rendering engine (README.template.md + variant metadata + readmeNarrative) for a single variant, invoked post-apply for promoted variants — closes the "post-promotion READMEs never regenerate" gap by reusing the same renderer that validate-templates.ts WS-08 and verify-readme-sync.ts already enforce. NOT wired into dev-sync (out of scope this PR). **D5 — skill graph generator extension** (`generate-skill-graph.ts` 1.0.1 → 1.1.0): extended to also scan templates/common/agents/ (previously discovered only L0 agents/ and variant templates/co-*/agents/) — dedup rule: if an agent name already exists as an L0 node (e.g. pm), keep the L0 node and skip the common-layer duplicate (commented in code). Expected result: +1 node (i18n-specialist, with used_by edges to its 3 required_skills) → **203 nodes** from 202; regenerated docs/skill-graph.json/.md. **D6 — fixture tests** (`tests/marker-rewrite.test.ts`): extended tests with 3 fixtures — (1) marker-zone with local modification → --marker-rewrite restores source content (overwrite verified); (2) intentional-duplicate section drifted → rewrite restores content AND refreshes hash (verify-adr-governance then passes); (3) idempotency: running --marker-rewrite twice → second run reports all in sync, no diff. All tests run via bun test tests/ and pass. **D7 — lifecycle bookkeeping**: script version bumps (+0.1.0 each) for propagate-to-templates.ts (2.4.0→2.5.0), verify-adr-governance.ts (1.3.0→1.4.0), generate-variant.ts (1.12.0→1.13.0), generate-skill-graph.ts (1.0.1→1.1.0), helpers/markers.ts (1.0.0 new); SCRIPTS.md rows updated (new helpers/markers.ts row + version/column changes for all 4 modified scripts + --marker-rewrite flag added to propagate-to-templates.ts); all modified scripts are L0-only per SCRIPTS.md layer column — no L1 propagation required, no lifecycle-sync-audit.ts Check X entries needed. **D8 — spec governance**: design doc docs/designs/2026-08-24-marker-propagation-engine-design.md created (header-table format copied from docs/designs/2026-08-24-i18n-specialist-suite-design.md; Status: implemented; scope = D1-D6 with pilot variant list and dedup rule) plus surgical docs/specs/registry.json edit (new entry 2026-08-24-marker-propagation-engine-design added; full-file rewrite avoided to preserve formatting — only the new entry appended). **D9 — CHANGELOG**: this entry (first bullet under [Unreleased] → Added, dated 2026-08-24, covering rewrite engine, 2 domains + pilot scope, shared parser, --regenerate flag, graph 202→203, fixtures, script version bumps). Verification battery all-pass: bun test tests/, bun scripts/audit.ts (all-pass incl. the drift gate rejects country marks in relation fields (extends the ADR-0057/0058 country_scoped_assets invariant); rejected alternatives recorded (hand-maintained registry, hard dependency semantics, per-layer graphs). `ADR-0061 Decision Record Standard`: the chain audit found Knowledge/Evidence/Rule layers present but the Decision link missing in EVERY variant — decisions vanish into chat transcripts; the standard introduces `docs/decisions/DEC-YYYYMMDD-NN.md` with fixed frontmatter (id/date/agent/decision/alternatives/evidence_refs[]/rules_applied[]/skills_used[]/status proposed|accepted|superseded) mutating by EXPLICIT SUPERSESSION never deletion; evidence standardizes as a common `evidence-ledger` skill + variant overlays (owner-confirmed; co-news keeps its 2-source rule as an overlay); hard gates get stable rule IDs (e.g. `NEWS-R1`); the PM Gateway gains a gate-moment rule (Design Gate Row 0 rulings, escalations, go/no-go MUST emit a decision record before dispatch continues); enforcement is prompt-level v1 (hook-level deferred until after 2 engagements). `ADR-0062 Marker-Based Doc Propagation Domains`: upgrades detect-then-reseed to detect-then-REWRITE via `propagate-to-templates.ts --marker-rewrite` covering both `<!-- COMMON-*:START/END -->` zones and `<!-- intentional-duplicate -->` markers (shared parser with verify-adr-governance.ts to avoid step-3.97 conflicts); two new whitelisted marker domains (`constitution-context` → templates/common/docs/context.md via the existing scrub transform; `variant-context` → each variant's context.md); pilot on co-news/co-hr/co-export/co-consult (owner-confirmed 4-variant scope) before extending to the remaining 7; `--regenerate <variant>` for the README engine and a WARN-then-error `translated_from_hash` gate for user-guide pairs; the wholesale `docs` propagation domain REMAINS disabled (this ADR is the whitelist/transform answer to propagation-map.json's pending decision, not a reversal). Constitution pointers added (ADR-0059 post-cutoff linkage): 06-skill-lifecycle (relations out of SKILLS.md → generated graph), 05-multi-agent-architecture §5.4 (gate-moment decision records), 00-ssot-architecture Three Types of Flows (marker-based domains). Verified: `verify-adr-governance --strict` exit 0 (6/6 post-cutoff ADRs linked, 9/9 markers in sync), `audit.ts` all-pass, `validate-templates` 0 errors (2 pre-existing co-deck WARNs), all new files Hangul-free. Implementation lands in PR2-PR9.

- **[2026-08-23]**: feat(scripts): **intentional-duplicate marker hashes — Stage 1b of ADR-0059, making constitution→template duplication drift-detectable (`verify-adr-governance.ts` 1.0.0 → 1.1.0).** The 9 `<!-- intentional-duplicate: workspace standards §N -->` markers (common context.md §3 Git/PR Workflow; co-abap/co-design/co-develop/co-game/co-security/co-work context.md §8 Coding Guidelines; `variant.context.template.md` ×2) marked sections maintained as local duplicates of constitution sections but carried no hash, so source drift was undetectable. Each marker now carries `source: docs/constitution/<NN>-<slug>.md; hash: <sha256-8>` (whole-file hash; §3 → `03-pr-workflow.md`, §8 → `08-coding-guidelines.md`, resolved generically by section-number prefix). The validator's new second phase recomputes and WARNs on mismatch ("source changed since hash was recorded — review the duplicated section and re-seed"), flags hashless markers, and reports `n/9 in sync`; a new `--update-marker-hashes` mode seeds/refreshes markers in place (mirrors `verify-readme-sync.ts --update-hashes` UX). Compatibility: `lifecycle-sync-audit.ts` Check D (informational duplicate registry) parses the old prefix unchanged — its capture regex already stopped at the marker's first `;`. Design note: the check lives in `verify-adr-governance.ts` itself rather than `verify-readme-sync.ts` (per ADR-0059 §2) — the README pipeline stays README-scoped and one governance validator owns both reflection checks behind the single `--governance-check` audit flag (no audit.ts change in this PR). Boundary: spans root scripts + 8 template marker files — documented exception to the Workspace & Template Boundary Policy (ADR-0057 precedent; same-session PR-A/PR-B pattern from earlier today). Verified: seeded 9/9 in sync; PM independent negative test — tampered hash → exactly one stale WARN (8/9) → restored → 9/9; `audit.ts --governance-check` all-pass; plain audit unchanged all-pass; `validate-templates` 0 errors (2 pre-existing co-deck WARNs); `lifecycle-sync-audit` 0 errors; `docs/constitution/` byte-identical after testing.
- **[2026-08-23]**: feat(scripts): **new `verify-adr-governance.ts` (1.0.0) + ADR-0059 — WARN-first detection for the upward-reflection gap the governance review found (ADR→constitution wiring was automated nowhere; ADR-0057's mechanism landed via #612 with zero constitution pointers, requiring manual #616).** The validator scans `docs/adr/*.md` (top level; parses frontmatter `status:`/`date:` with a `**Status**:` body-line fallback, handling the mixed pre-frontmatter formats and value variants like `"Accepted"`/`Accepted (Amended …)`/`active`), applies an in-file cutoff (2026-08-23 — ADRs dated on/after it require linkage, earlier ones grandfathered to avoid ~50-file backfill noise), and WARNs when an Accepted/active ADR is referenced by neither its canonical `ADR-00NN` form nor its filename anywhere in the governance corpus (CONSTITUTION.md + `docs/constitution/` + `docs/governance/`, 31 files). WARN exits 0; exit 1 only on operational failure. Wired into `audit.ts` (2.19.1→2.20.0) behind `--governance-check`, mirroring the ADR-0055 `--spec-check` Stage-1 burn-in pattern (dev-sync wiring = Stage 2 once clean); L1 `audit.ts` mirror propagated + both `SCRIPTS.md` rows aligned (lifecycle Check B). `docs/constitution/09-operations-workflow.md` documents the new audit flag — and the validator dogfooded itself on first run: ADR-0059 initially WARNed as unlinked and got its constitution pointer (3/3 linked after). ADR-0059 also records the rejected alternatives (frontmatter retrofit + 58-file backfill, constitution last-reviewed footers + 12-month WARN, auto-generated ADR index, auto-writing governance prose, ERROR severity on day one) and stages the intentional-duplicate marker hashes as Stage 1b inside `verify-adr-governance.ts` itself (not a separate validator). PM review corrected two factual errors in the agent-authored ADR draft (marker locations: template context.md files, not variant READMEs; PR #616's actual file list). Verified: fixture round-trip (unlinked ADR-9999 → WARN + exit 0 → deleted → clean), `audit.ts --governance-check` all-pass, plain `audit.ts` unchanged all-pass, `validate-templates` 0 errors (2 pre-existing co-deck WARNs), `lifecycle-sync-audit` clean.

### Fixed
- **[2026-08-24]**: fix(scripts): **PR7 marker-rewrite engine post-review fixes — four defects found by PM verification before merge (`propagate-to-templates.ts` 2.5.0 → 2.5.1, `generate-skill-graph.ts` 1.1.0 underscore exclusion, `propagation-map.json` 1.5.0 → 1.6.0 scrub flag).** (1) `--domain` was silently ignored in `--marker-rewrite` mode — unknown names fell through to all-domains instead of erroring; now validated against propagation-map.json (exit 1 + available-domains stderr) and a known name restricts the marker-inject loop (caught by the new fixture test). (2) Cross-domain zone pollution + first-line-substring source pairing made every pilot `COMMON-CONTEXT` zone permanently skippable ("no matching source section") — the `variant-context` domain did not function; zones are now filtered to the domain's own marker and paired to source zones by document order, zones rewritten bottom-up so splices cannot shift later line numbers. (3) CRLF double-conversion (`\n`→`\r\n` applied to content already containing `\r\n` produced `\r\r\n`) fixed in both the zone and intentional-duplicate write paths via LF-normalize-then-reapply; dry-run summary now says "Would overwrite" instead of "Overwritten". (4) The constitution-context domain promised scrubbed output but the engine copied source slices verbatim, leaking a `docs/constitution/06-skill-lifecycle.md` part-file link into `templates/common/docs/context.md` — new `scrub_constitution_refs` domain flag applies `scrubConstitutionRefs()` to zone content, plus scrub rule A-5 rewriting part-file markdown links to `[docs/context.md](docs/context.md)` (A-2 already covers links whose text mentions CONSTITUTION.md; script-parity comparisons use the comment-only code branch, so no L0↔L1 pair is affected). Also: the new common-agent graph scan counted `templates/common/agents/_COMMON.md` (directory README, not an agent) as a node (204 vs designed 203) — underscore-prefixed files now excluded, verified 203 nodes/533 edges; `_COMMON.md`'s stale "Only `pm.md` exists" line updated to include `i18n-specialist.md`. Post-fix state: marker-rewrite idempotent (re-run: 0 would-overwrite, 23 in sync, 0 skipped), full battery green (audit all-pass, validate-templates 0 errors, verify-adr-governance --strict 9/9, verify-skill-graph exit 0, --check-drift clean, validate-md-language exit 0, 0 Hangul on added lines, 0 `docs/constitution/` leaks in the templates diff). Deviations adjudicated and documented in the design doc's Deviations section (CONSTITUTION.md source-side marker pair — 2 inert comment lines; 11 variant AGENTS.md zone refreshes = stale `l2-`→`l3-to-variant-pipeline.ts` rename correction inside engine-owned COMMON-AGENTS zones).
- **[2026-08-24]**: fix(scripts): **`generate-skill-graph.ts` 1.0.0 → 1.0.1 — fenced-code-block bug in prose reference extraction (PR2 follow-up found during PR3 verification).** `extractBacktickReferences()` ran its inline backtick regex over full file content, so the ``` delimiters of fenced code blocks were consumed as if they were inline backtick pairs — swallowing the fenced content and misaligning the pairing for every backtick reference after the first fence in a file. Any SKILL.md or agent file with a code fence above prose references silently lost those advisory `references` edges. Fix: fenced blocks are stripped (`/^```[\s\S]*?^```/gm`) before extraction. Regenerated graph: **431 → 513 edges** (+82 previously-suppressed advisory prose references restored; all advisory per ADR-0060, node count unchanged at 199, `docs/skill-graph.md` legitimately unchanged — the human catalog doesn't enumerate references edges). Spec registry entry `2026-08-24-skill-relationship-graph-infrastructure-design` last_updated refreshed (follow-up implementation activity on the same spec). Verified: verify-skill-graph exit 0 (199/513), validate-templates 0 errors (2 pre-existing co-deck WARNs), lifecycle-sync-audit all checks passed.

- **[2026-08-24]**: fix(scripts): **spec-exempt delivery + variant-safe audit import + CRLF-tolerant nul lint — three fixes ported back from the co-abap remediation (`Projects/co-abap/docs/upstream-fix-list.md` §4–§6).** (1) `dev-sync.ts` 1.7.1 → **1.7.2**: `--spec-exempt` was interpolated into the Bun `$` shell as `" --spec-exempt=X"` — a single argv word whose leading space defeats audit's `startsWith('--spec-exempt=')` parse — making the ADR-0055 Stage 2 escape hatch silently inert on every `/sync`; the exemption is now delivered via the documented `SYNC_SPEC_EXEMPT` environment fallback. (2) `audit.ts` 2.21.0 → **2.21.1**: the L0-only validators wiring statically imported `'./validators/index.ts'`, which tsc statically resolves in every variant checkout (TS2307) despite the runtime `existsSync` guard — switched to a non-literal `new URL('./validators/index.ts', import.meta.url)` specifier so variants type-check while runtime resolution is unchanged; also made nul-lint `stripComment()` CRLF-tolerant by stripping a trailing `\r` before matching — under autocrlf checkouts (any variant without `.gitattributes` `eol=lf`) the `$` anchors never matched and the scanner flagged its own prose comments mentioning `> nul`. Both verified end-to-end in co-abap (typecheck exit 0 without exclusions; full audit PASS incl. 99-file nul lint). Registries bumped in root + L1 SCRIPTS.md; L1 script copies propagated.
- **[2026-08-24]**: fix(scripts): **dev-sync.ts 1.7.0 → 1.7.1 — resolved the last 7 tsc errors in the core pipeline script (typing-only, zero behavior change).** `ShellResult.stderr` is typed `Buffer` by bun-types but is a string at runtime, so the two `.stderr.trim()` sites (project-tests warning at ~line 265, skill-sync warning at ~line 333) now coerce via `String(...)` first; the five `withRetry` call-site lambdas typed `(r: { exitCode: number }) => boolean` violated the contravariant `(result: unknown) => boolean` contract declared by retry-handler's `isSuccess` option and are rewritten as unknown-safe predicates (`typeof r === "object" && r !== null && (r as { exitCode: number }).exitCode === 0`). With this patch the workspace-root core pipeline script type-checks clean under strict mode, so downstream variants inherit an error-free `dev-sync.ts` on their next `upgrade-project` reconciliation — co-abap had documented these exact 7 patches as upstream debt in its `docs/upstream-fix-list.md` §1 (with a temporary tsconfig exclusion that can be dropped once this propagates). Verified: patch is parse-clean and module-imports under bun from its own root; the 8 failures observed when running `bun test scripts` from root all originate in nested variant projects (`Projects/co-deck`, `Projects/co-game` security-validator suites + a visual-regression baseline) and pre-date this change — untouched here.

- **[2026-08-23]**: fix(scripts): **closed the body-drift blind spot in `verify-readme-sync.ts` (1.1.1 → 1.2.0) and completed the ADR-0013 removal of inert `PLACEHOLDER` hash fields.** The static audit only compared EN `content_hash` vs KO `translated_from_hash` — never either against the actual README body — so a body edit that skipped the hash refresh left both hashes equally stale and passed silently. v1.2.0 adds a drift gate to `runStaticAudit`: when a pair matches and tracking is active, the stored EN hash is recomputed against the current body and a mismatch FAILs with both hashes and the fix command; the `PLACEHOLDER` sentinel and absent-field cases stay PASS ("tracking not active", ADR-0013). Since pre-commit always runs the static audit when READMEs are staged, body drift is now commit-blocked. **The gate caught 2 real defects on first run**: co-develop (`0057c576…` → `2aa680da…`) and co-game (`907baf6d…` → `a8f2cf58…`) carried hashes written from a stale body state by #617's resync; both EN hashes refreshed via the maintainer path and KO `translated_from_hash` mirrored — live proof the check works, no synthetic negative test needed. **ADR-0013 completion**: removed the dead sentinel fields from the 4 root index READMEs (`README.md` `content_hash`; `README_ko/es/ja.md` `translated_from_hash`), `templates/common/README_ko.md`, and the two scaffold templates `templates/common/docs/README.template.md` / `README_ko.template.md` (which stamped `PLACEHOLDER` into every newly generated variant README) — root/common now uniformly report "tracking not active" (deliberate: activating root hash tracking would hard-fail the WS-12 translation-lag allowance on `README_es/ja`), and new variants start untracked until a maintainer activates tracking via `--update-hashes`. `sync_version` retained everywhere; `Projects/*` copies are separate repositories, out of scope. L0+L1 sync: script published to `templates/common/scripts/`, `scripts/SCRIPTS.md` + L1 mirror registry bumped to 1.2.0. Gates: `verify-readme-sync` 13/13 PASS, `validate-templates` 0 errors (2 pre-existing co-deck WARNs), `audit.ts` all-pass, `lifecycle-sync-audit` clean.
- **[2026-08-23]**: docs(constitution): **wired the country-profile mechanism into the governance documents (PR-E, closing out the A→D migration's documentation debt).** The PR-A mechanism (#612) landed with ADR-0057 + the convention doc but without pointers from the constitution/contract layer, so an agent reading only the governance docs would never discover it. Five surgical additions: **(1)** constitution §7.3.5 *Target Country Selection* — the scaffold-time `--country` flow (supported-code validation, `Target-Jurisdiction` provenance line, `template-version.txt` `country=` row, `docs/countries/ACTIVE.md` pointer), region-neutral as the `--yes`/CI default, and the post-overlay pruning of `country_scoped_assets` skills from all 4 mirror directories via `prune-country-scoped-assets.ts` (same helper in `create-l2-scaffold.ts`); **(2)** constitution §4.3 *Country ≠ Language* — `i18n.locale_codes` and `country_config` are independent axes (CH → de/fr/it; `zh-CN`/`zh-TW` are language+region hybrids, never country codes; a profile's Language section references locale_codes, never redefines it; language skills must never be country-scoped); **(3)** constitution §6.8 *Country-Scoped Skills* — registry-only design (no SKILL.md frontmatter field), the deployment/pruning rule, the agents-reference-scoped-skills-only-via-country-profile rule (no `required_skills` — pruning would strand dangling refs), and the validator's registry-integrity + dangling-ref checks; **(4)** `docs/governance/variant-contract.md` gains the *Country Profile Standard* section (country_config shape, `default: null` policy, 5-section profile format, Check 2 `country-config`/`country-scoped-assets` enforcement, backtick language convention) and a `docs/countries/<CODE>.md` row in the Optional Files table (currently co-hr/co-export/co-news/co-consult); **(5)** `templates/common/docs/context.md` gains a *Country Profiles* pointer subsection under Documentation Standards so every scaffolded project's immutable context references `ACTIVE.md` and the convention doc. Verified: `validate-templates` 0 errors (2 pre-existing co-deck WARNs), `audit.ts` all-pass (a double-backticked Korean statute example in variant-contract.md tripped the language gate on first run — replaced with a prose description, the same double-backtick trap PR-D hit in KR.md).

### Fixed
- **[2026-08-24]**: fix(scripts): **country-profile upgrade/pipeline gaps — `upgrade-project.ts` 1.10.0 → 1.10.1 + `l3-to-variant-pipeline.ts` 1.12.0 → 1.12.1: the two upgrade-path defects (G9) found by the country-profile reflection audit, plus the never-implemented PR-A changelog claim.** (1) **F1 — skill re-injection**: the SYNC_IF_NEWER skills pass copied every `templates/common/skills/` update into existing projects with no country check, so a non-KR project upgrading after a k-* skill update re-received the exact skills scaffold-time pruning removes; a new registry-driven "COUNTRY-SCOPED SKILL PRUNE (ADR-0057/0058)" pass runs after both skill-copy sites and deletes re-injected `country_scoped_assets.skills` from all 4 mirror directories unless the detected country matches (conflict guard: content-identical to the common/variant template → prune; locally modified → kept with a `⚠️ CONFLICT` warning; recoverable via the pre-upgrade stash). (2) **F2 — country= erasure**: the post-upgrade `template-version.txt` rewrite dropped the `country=` provenance line, silently downgrading KR projects to region-neutral on the next upgrade; country is now detected pre-upgrade (template-version.txt `country=` authoritative including `none`, with `ACTIVE.md` "Active jurisdiction:" fallback) and rewritten in new-project.ts field order (`variant/version/platform/country/upgraded`). (3) **Pipeline fork gap**: PR-A's changelog claimed `l3-to-variant-pipeline.ts` would "refuse scoped-skill fork copies" but no such code existed (reconcileFile only discards identical files — the co-consult `k-law` fork is historical proof); new PHASE 2.5 loads the registry (existsSync-guarded) and keeps country-scoped skills out of the variant fork ("stays in templates/common — not forked"), filtering the extracted skill manifest for consistency. (4) Constitution stale refs: `create-l2-scaffold.ts` → `create-l3-scaffold.ts` (renamed in 60089b65) at `00-ssot-architecture.md` + 4 sites in `06.5-script-lifecycle.md` (also fixed the `generate-l2-readme.ts` rename). Verified: E2E neutral upgrade (re-injection proven — 3 NEW k-* logged — then pruned) + KR fixture keeps all scoped skills; `verify-country-prune` 4/4; `test-l3-to-variant-promotion` 11/11 (KR fixture keepInVariant 13 → 12); `audit.ts` all-pass; `validate-templates` 0 errors (2 pre-existing co-deck WARNs).
